THE IMPACT OF AI-DRIVEN STRATEGIES IN SOCIAL MEDIA MARKETING
Dr U. Kanmani.
Assistant Professor,
Department of Commerce,
Sri Ram College of Arts & Science, Perumalpattu-602024
Abstract
This study examines the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in shaping social
media marketing strategies and outcomes. As businesses increasingly leverage AI
technologies to enhance their digital presence, understanding the impact of these tools on
marketing effectiveness and consumer engagement becomes crucial. This research
synthesizes recent literature and case studies to provide a comprehensive overview of AI
applications in social media marketing, including predictive analytics, persona lized content
creation, chatbots, and automated campaign optimization.  The study reveals that AI -driven
strategies significantly improve targeting precision, content relevance, and real-time customer
interactions, leading to enhanced engagement rates and c onversion metrics. However, it also
highlights potential challenges, such as data privacy concerns and the need for human
oversight to maintain brand authenticity. The findings suggest that while AI offers powerful
capabilities for optimizing social media marketing efforts, successful implementation requires
a balanced approach that integrates AI tools with human creativity and strategic insight.  This
research contributes to the growing body of knowledge on digital marketing innovation and
provides practica l implications for marketers seeking to leverage AI in their social media
strategies. Future research directions are proposed to further explore the long -term effects of
AI adoption on brand perception and customer loyalty in the social media landscape.

Keywords: Artificial Intelligence, Social Media Marketing, Digital Strategy, Consumer
Engagement, Marketing Automation

I. Introduction
In recent years, artificial intelligence (AI) has become a game -changer in the field of
marketing, revolutionizing the wa y businesses engage with consumers and make strategic
decisions. By leveraging vast amounts of data, AI enables marketers to gain deeper insights
into consumer behavior, predict trends, and personalize interactions at an unprecedented
scale. From automatin g routine tasks to optimizing complex marketing campaigns, AI tools
such as machine learning algorithms, natural language processing, and data analytics are
enhancing efficiency and effectiveness across all aspects of marketing.  Social media has
become a vital platform for brands to connect with their audiences, and the integration of AI
in social media marketing is transforming how these connections are made. AI -driven tools
are now being used to automate content creation, schedule posts, and even interact  with
followers through chatbots. Moreover, AI's ability to analyze vast amounts of social data
allows marketers to gain insights into consumer sentiment, monitor trends in real -time, and
optimize ad targeting with remarkable precision. This synergy betwee n AI and social media
is enabling brands to deliver more relevant, timely, and personalized experiences to their
audiences, ultimately driving engagement and brand loyalty.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 321

II. Understanding AI in Social Media Marketing
Definition and Explanation of AI in the Context of Social Media
Artificial Intelligence (AI) in social media marketing refers to the use of advanced algorithms
and data -driven technologies to automate, enhance, and optimize various aspects of social
media management and marketing. AI enables  marketers to analyze vast amounts of social
data, predict user behaviors, and personalize content and ads in ways that were previously
unimaginable. By processing and interpreting data faster and more accurately than human
capabilities, AI helps brands create more targeted campaigns, improve customer engagement,
and measure the impact of their social media efforts more effectively. In essence, AI acts as a
powerful tool that transforms raw social data into actionable insights and automates routine
tasks, allowing marketers to focus on strategic decision-making.
Key AI Technologies Used in Social Media Marketing
1. Machine Learning (ML):
Machine learning involves training algorithms to recognize patterns and make data -driven
decisions. In social media marketing, ML is used for predictive analytics, such as forecasting
trends and identifying the best times to post content. It also helps in segmenting audiences
and personalizing ad campaigns to enhance their effectiveness.
2. Natural Language Processing (NLP):
NLP enables machines to understand and interpret human language, making it essential for
analyzing social media conversations, comments, and messages. It allows marketers to
perform sentiment analysis, detect trends, and gain insights into consumer opinion s and
emotions. NLP is also used in chatbots to provide human -like interactions in customer
service.
3. Computer Vision:
This AI technology allows systems to interpret and analyze visual content, such as images
and videos. In social media marketing, computer vision is used to recognize brand logos,
monitor visual trends, and even automate the tagging of products in user-generated content.
4. Chatbots and Virtual Assistants:
AI-powered chatbots and virtual assistants are used on social media platforms to engage wi th
users in real -time. They can answer queries, provide recommendations, and even guide
customers through purchasing processes, enhancing user experience and increasing
conversion rates.
5. Recommendation Engines:
These AI systems analyze user behavior and pr eferences to recommend relevant content,
products, or services. On social media platforms, recommendation engines help deliver
personalized content feeds and suggest pages, posts, or ads that align with individual
interests.
6. Automated Content Creation and Curation:
AI tools can generate social media posts, headlines, and even entire articles based on
predefined parameters. They can also curate content from various sources to keep social
media pages active and engaging, saving time and resources for marketers.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 322

III. Automating Social Media Posts with AI
A. Content Creation
AI-Powered Content Generators:
AI content generators, like GPT -based models, can create social media posts, blog
summaries, and even full -length articles based on specific keywords or themes . These tools
help marketers maintain a consistent posting schedule while ensuring that the content remains
relevant and engaging. By analyzing user interactions and trending topics, AI can suggest
post ideas that resonate with target audiences.
Automated Image and Video Creation:
AI tools such as Canva's AI-powered design assistant or video editing platforms like Lumen5
can generate visual content quickly and efficiently. These tools can create branded images,
infographics, and videos tailored to specific platforms or audiences, making it easier for
marketers to produce high-quality visual content without extensive design skills.
B. Content Curation
AI Algorithms for Finding and Sharing Relevant Content:
AI-powered content curation tools like Feedly or Cura ta use machine learning to sift through
vast amounts of online content to find articles, videos, and other media that are relevant to a
brand's audience. These tools help marketers share valuable, up -to-date content with
followers, keeping their social media pages active and engaging.
C. Scheduling and Timing
AI-Driven Optimal Posting Times:
AI tools like Hootsuite or Buffer analyze user engagement data to determine the best times to
post content. By understanding when an audience is most active, these tools help increase the
visibility and reach of social media posts.
Automated Scheduling Tools:
Automated scheduling tools allow marketers to plan and queue posts in advance, ensuring a
consistent presence on social media. These tools can automatically adjust the posting
schedule based on real-time data, optimizing content delivery for maximum engagement.
IV. AI-Powered Social Media Advertising
A. Ad Creation
Automated Ad Copy Generation:
AI-driven platforms like Copy.ai or Writesonic can generate compelling ad copy based on a
few input keywords or descriptions. This automation accelerates the ad creation process and
helps marketers produce multiple variations of ad text for A/B testing.
AI-Assisted Image and Video Ad Creation:
Tools like Adobe Sensei and Promo.com use AI to streamline the creation of visuals for ads.
These platforms can suggest layouts, select appropria te stock images or videos, and even edit
content to fit the desired format and style.
B. Audience Targeting
AI Algorithms for Identifying Ideal Audiences:
AI algorithms analyze user data to identify the most relevant audiences for a particular ad
campaign. By examining factors such as demographics, online behavior, and past
interactions, these algorithms help marketers reach potential customers who are more likely
to convert.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 323

Predictive Analytics for Audience Behavior:
Predictive analytics tools use histori cal data to forecast future behaviors and preferences of
target audiences. This information enables marketers to tailor their messaging and product
offerings to meet the anticipated needs of their audience, improving campaign effectiveness.
C. Ad Performance Optimization
Real-Time Bid Adjustments:
AI-powered bidding strategies, such as those offered by Google Ads and Facebook Ads,
automatically adjust ad bids in real -time based on performance metrics. This ensures that ad
spend is allocated efficiently, maximizing the return on investment (ROI).
A/B Testing Automation:
AI automates the process of A/B testing by dynamically creating and testing multiple ad
variations. It identifies which combinations of copy, visuals, and targeting perform best,
allowing marketers to optimize their campaigns without manual intervention.
V. Benefits of AI in Social Media Marketing
Increased Efficiency and Productivity:
AI automates time -consuming tasks such as content creation, scheduling, and performance
monitoring, freeing up marketers to focus on strategy and creative development. This boosts
productivity and allows for more efficient campaign management.
Enhanced Personalization and Targeting:
AI's ability to analyze and segment large datasets enables highly personalized mar keting. By
delivering tailored content and ads to specific audience segments, marketers can improve
engagement rates and foster stronger connections with their audiences.
Improved ROI Through Data-Driven Decisions:
AI tools provide deep insights into audie nce behavior, campaign performance, and market
trends, empowering marketers to make informed decisions. This data -driven approach leads
to more effective strategies, optimized ad spend, and ultimately, a higher return on
investment.
VI. Challenges and Considerations
A. Maintaining Brand Voice and Authenticity
While AI is powerful in automating content creation and engagement, it can sometimes
struggle to capture the nuances of a brand’s unique voice and personality. Over -reliance on
AI-generated content can  lead to messages that feel generic or impersonal, potentially

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 324

alienating customers. It is crucial for marketers to balance AI -driven automation with human
input to ensure that the brand's messaging remains consistent, authentic, and reflective of its
values and tone.
B. Data Privacy and Ethical Concerns
The use of AI in social media marketing often involves collecting and analyzing large
amounts of user data, which raises significant privacy and ethical issues. Marketers must
navigate regulations like the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) and the California
Consumer Privacy Act (CCPA) to ensure that they are compliant in their data handling
practices. Additionally, there is an ethical responsibility to be transparent with users about
how their data is being used and to avoid intrusive or manipulative marketing tactics.
C. The Need for Human Oversight and Creativity
Although AI can handle many aspects of social media marketing, human creativity and
strategic thinking remain irreplaceable. AI tools lac k the ability to fully understand cultural
context, humor, or complex emotional nuances, which are often key elements in crafting
impactful marketing messages. Human oversight is essential to interpret AI -generated
insights, curate content, and develop cre ative strategies that resonate on a deeper level with
audiences. Marketers must strike a balance between leveraging AI for efficiency and using
human creativity to craft engaging, authentic, and memorable brand experiences.
VII. Future Trends in AI and Social Media Marketing
A. Emerging Technologies
1. AR/VR Integration with AI in Social Media Marketing:
The convergence of augmented reality (AR) and virtual reality (VR) with AI is set to
transform how brands engage with consumers on social media. AI -powered AR  filters and
VR experiences can provide immersive and interactive ways for users to engage with
products and brands. For instance, AI can be used to personalize virtual try -ons, simulate in-
store experiences, or create interactive storytelling campaigns th at merge the digital and
physical worlds. As AR/VR technology becomes more accessible, we can expect an increase
in AI -driven experiential marketing campaigns on platforms like Instagram, Snapchat, and
TikTok.
2. AI-Driven Influencer Marketing Platforms:
AI is increasingly being used to identify and manage influencer partnerships. Advanced AI
algorithms can analyze vast amounts of social media data to find influencers whose audiences
align with a brand’s target demographic. These platforms can predict the potential reach and
impact of collaborations, making influencer marketing more strategic and data -driven. As AI
technology advances, expect these platforms to become more sophisticated, incorporating

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 325

sentiment analysis and predictive modeling to optimize influencer relationships and campaign
outcomes.
3. Enhanced Social Listening and Sentiment Analysis:
Future advancements in natural language processing (NLP) will enable more nuanced social
listening capabilities, allowing brands to understand not just what is  being said about them,
but also the underlying emotions and context of these conversations. AI will be able to detect
subtle changes in consumer sentiment in real time, helping brands respond proactively to
potential crises or capitalize on emerging trends.
4. Voice and Conversational AI in Social Media:
With the rise of voice search and voice -enabled devices, integrating conversational AI into
social media marketing is a growing trend. AI -powered voice bots and virtual assistants can
engage with users on soc ial platforms, answer queries, and even facilitate shopping
experiences directly through voice commands. As voice technology continues to evolve, it
will open up new avenues for customer interaction and brand storytelling.
B. Predictions for the Evolution of AI in Social Media
1. Hyper-Personalized Marketing Campaigns:
As AI continues to advance, the ability to deliver hyper -personalized content and
advertisements will become even more refined. AI will leverage deeper insights from user
behavior, preferences, and interactions to create highly tailored experiences, such as
personalized video messages or dynamic ads that adapt in real time based on viewer
engagement. This level of personalization will enhance user experience and increase the
effectiveness of marketing campaigns.
2. Automated Creative Generation and Optimization:
Future AI tools will not only automate content creation but also optimize creative elements in
real time based on performance data. For example, AI could dynamically adjust ad visuals,
headlines, or calls to action during a campaign to maximize engagement and conversion
rates. This level of automation and optimization will make marketing more agile and
responsive to audience preferences.
3. Increased Use of Predictive Analytics for Strategic Planning:
Predictive analytics powered by AI will play a larger role in strategic planning for social
media marketing. By analyzing past campaign data and current market trends, AI will help
marketers forecast future performance and identify optimal strategies for content, advertising,
and audience engagement. This proactive approach will enable brands to stay ahead of the
curve and make data-informed decisions more effectively.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 326

4. Greater Emphasis on Ethical AI and Responsible Data Use:
As AI becomes more integrated into social media marketing, there will be a growing focus on
ethical AI practices and responsible data use. Brands will need to be more transparent about
how AI is used in marketing and ensure that their practices comply with privacy regulations
and ethical standards. This shift will likely lead to the development of new guidelines and
best practices for AI-driven marketing.
VIII. Conclusion
AI has fundamentally transformed social media marketing by enhancing efficiency,
personalization, and strategic decision-making. From automating content creation and
optimizing ad targeting to providing deep insights through predictive analytics, AI empowers
marketers to engage audiences in more meaningful and impactful ways. It has enabled brands
to deliver highly tailored experiences, streamline operations, and achieve better returns on
investment. However, the integration of AI also presents challenges, such as maintaining
brand authenticity and navigating complex ethical and privacy considerations. As AI
technology continues to evolve, it offers immense potential to revolutionize the marketing
landscape even further. Marketers should embrace these tools to stay competitive, but they
must do so with a responsible and ethical approach. This involves ensuring transpa rency in
data usage, safeguarding user privacy, and balancing automation with human creativity and
oversight. By leveraging AI thoughtfully and ethically, marketers can harness its full
potential to build stronger, more authentic connections with their aud iences and drive
sustainable business growth.

The Integration of E-Commerce and Digital Marketing: Strategies for Customer
Engagement and Online Business Growth
J. Imran Basha, B.Com (CA) & J. Dinesh Kumar, B.Com (CA)
Sriram College of Arts and Science
Perumalpattu
Abstract
E-commerce and digital marketing have transformed the way businesses reach
customers and sell products and services. E -commerce enables online buying and selling,
while digital marketing uses platforms such as social media, search engines, email, and
websites to attract and engage customers. Together, they help businesses expand their market
reach, improve customer relationships, and increase sales. This study highlights the
importance of e -commerce and digital marketing in modern business and their role in
achieving growth and competitiveness.
Keywords: E-Commerce - Digital Marketing - Online Business - Customer Journey - Search
Engine Optimization - Social Media Marketing - Digital Payments - Data Analytics -
Customer Experience - Online Retail.
Introduction
E-Commerce refers to the buying and selling of products or services through
electronic networks, mainly the Internet. It in cludes online stores, marketplaces, digital
payment systems, order management and electronic customer service. Digital Marketing
refers to the promotion of products, services and brands through digital channels such as
search engines, websites, social media, email and online advertising.
Objectives
 To understand the concept and importance of E-Commerce and Digital Marketing.
 To study Digital Marketing helps businesses reach customers and increase sales.
 To identify the benefits and challenges of E-Commerce in modern business.
 To understand the role digital payments and security in online transactions.
Review of Literature
Chaffey ed al., ( 2022) Provide a comprehensive framework for digital marketing in
the modern era. The authors explain the evolution from t raditional marketing to digital -first
strategies, emphasizing the importance of the RACE framework (Reach, Act, Convert,
Engage) for e -commerce success. They argue that data -driven marketing, content strategy,
and marketing automation are critical for cust omer acquisition and retention in online
business.
Laudon ed al. , (2023) Present e -commerce as a combination of business models,
technology infrastructure, and social impact. Their 16th edition highlights the post -pandemic
boom in B2C and B2B e-commerce, the dominance of mobile commerce (m-commerce), and

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 328

the role of AI, cloud computing, and social networks in transforming online transactions.
They state that understanding consumer be haviour in digital environments is key to e -
commerce sustainability.
Kingsnorth ed al., ( 2022) Emphasizes an integrated  approach to online marketing.
According to him, a successful e -commerce business cannot depend on a single channel.
SEO, PPC, social media, email, and content marketing must work together in a unified
strategy. He also discusses the importance of customer persona, user experience (UX), and
analytics for measuring digital marketing performance.
Kotler ed al., ( 2017) Explain the shift from traditional to digital marketing as
Marketing 4.0. The study argues that in a connected world, customer journeys have become
non-linear, combining online and offline touchpoints. The authors introduce the concept of
customer advocacy, where e -commerce brands must build human -centric engagement
through authenticity, conversations, and community building.
Strauss ed al., ( 2021) Define e -marketing as the use of electronic communication
technology to achieve marketing objectives. Their book details online branding, pricing
strategies in e -commerce, and the role of social media and onli ne customer relationship
management (e-CRM). They conclude that e -marketing enables global reach with lower cost
and higher personalization compared to traditional marketing.
E-Commerce
E-Commerce is the process of conducting commercial activities through electronic
networks. It allows customers to search for products, compare alternatives, place orders,
make payments and receive products or services through digital systems. E -Commerce can
include business -to-consumer, business -to-business, consumer -to-consumer and consumer -
to-business transactions.
E-Commerce operations
E-Commerce operations include product listing, inventory management, order
processing, payment confirmation, packaging, delivery, returns and customer service. Smooth
coordination between th ese activities is essential because digital customers expect accurate
information, convenient payment, reliable delivery and quick support. Technology helps
businesses automate many routine activities and monitor orders throughout the fulfilment
process.
Digital Marketing
Digital Marketing is the use of digital technologies and online communication
channels to promote products, services and brands. It focuses on reaching relevant audiences,
creating useful content, encouraging engagement and measuring custo mer responses. Unlike
traditional marketing, digital marketing can provide detailed information about audience
interaction and campaign performance.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 329

The Digital Customer Journey
The digital customer journey can be understood as a sequence of discovery,
consideration, decision, purchase and post -purchase engagement. A customer may first
discover a product through search, social media or online content. The customer then
explores product information, reviews, price and alternatives before deciding whether to
purchase. After payment, delivery and customer support influence satisfaction, repeat
purchases and recommendations.
Digital Payments & Security
Digital payments make online transactions convenient through methods such as cards,
bank transfers, mobile payments and other electronic payment systems. Security is essential
because E -Commerce involves financial and personal information. Businesses should use
secure payment processes, appropriate authentication, responsible data handling and clear
privacy practices to build customer trust.
Data Analytics & Personalization
Digital platforms generate data about customer visits, searches, product views,
purchases and interactions Analytics helps businesses understand customer behaviour and
measure marketing perfo rmance. Businesses can use these insights to improve website
design, campaign effectiveness, product recommendations and customer communication.
Personalization should be used responsibly and should respect customer privacy and consent.
Challenges
Major challenges include intense competition, cybersecurity risks, privacy concerns,
changing customer expectations, delivery problems, online fraud, platform dependence and
the need for continuous technological improvement. Businesses also need to maintain
accurate product information, reliable customer service and consistent digital experiences
across different devices and channels.
Emerging Trends
Important developments include artificial intelligence, conversational commerce,
mobile-first experiences, social co mmerce, voice and visual search, automation, advanced
analytics and increasingly personalized customer experiences. Businesses are also focusing
on faster fulfilment, integrated digital payments and stronger data protection. These trends are
changing in how customers discover, evaluate and purchase products online.
Findings
The study shows that E -Commerce and Digital Marketing are closely connected
rather than separate business activities. Digital Marketing supports customer acquisition and
engagement, whil e E -Commerce supports transaction and fulfilment. Customer trust,

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 330

usability, secure payments, relevant communication, reliable delivery and data-based decision
making are important for creating a successful digital customer experience.
Conclusion
E-Commerce and Digital Marketing have transformed the way businesses
communicate with customers and conduct commercial activities. Digital Marketing helps a
business attract and engage customers, while E -Commerce enables customers to complete
transactions through digital platforms. Their integration creates a connected customer journey
from discovery to purchase and post -purchase support. As technology continues to develop,
businesses that focus on customer experience, responsible data use, security and continuous
improvement can build stronger digital operations and relationships.

Artificial Intelligence as the Fundamental Pillar of Modern  Business and Commerce
R. POOVARASAN & V. LOKESH,
B.COM(CA)
SRI RAM COLLEGE OF ARTS AND SCIENCE
PERUMALPATTU
Abstract
Artificial intelligence has evolved from an operational optimizer into the core engine
of modern commercial strategy. This abstract examines machine learning, predictive
analytics, and autonomous systems are fundamentally altering the speed, structure, and  scale
of global business. At the enterprise level, AI shifts decision -making from retrospective
analysis to Realtime predictive execution. In commerce, hyper -personalized customer
journeys, dynamic pricing models, and autonomous supply chains are redefini ng consumer
expectations and competitive advantage. Beyond efficiency, AI is fostering entirely new
business models, enabling hyper -lean organizations to operate with global reach and
facilitating dynamic, multi-agent automated transactions.
Keywords: Artificial intelligence – business- commerce, Future- retail- Intelligent- customer
service.
Introduction
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is rapidly transforming the way commerce and businesses
operate. From automated customer service and personalized recommendations to data
analysis, fraud detection, and supply chain management, AI is becoming an important part of
modern business. It enables organizations to process large amounts of information quickly,
make data-driven decisions, reduce operational costs, and improve customer experience. The
future of commerce is likely to involve greater collaboration between h umans and intelligent
technologies. Businesses may increasingly use AI to understand consumer behaviors predict
market trends, automate routine tasks, and develop new products and services. At the same
time, the growing use of AI raises important concerns about employment, data privacy,
cybersecurity, transparency, and ethical decision -making. Therefore, AI is not simply a
technological development; it is a major force shaping the future of commerce and business.
Companies that understand their opportunitie s and manage their risks responsible will be
better positioned to adapt to an increasingly digital and competitive business environment.
Research Objectives
•  To understand the concept and importance of Artificial Intelligence in modern business.
•  To examine how AI is transforming traditional business and commercial activities.
Reviews of Literature
1.The literature on Artificial Intelligence (AI) in commerce and business highlights its
evolution from experimental technology to a core driver of business strategy.1. IBM (2026)
in its article "What is Artificial Intelligence (AI) in Business?" defines AI in business as the
use of technologies such as machine learning, NLP, and computer vision to automate work

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 332

and improve decision-making. The study reports that 79% of executives say AI has improved
productivity and will significantly contribute to revenue by 2030, but only 24% clearly
understand its revenue source. This gap shows that while benefits are visible, aligning AI
with financial outcomes remains a challenge.
2. Survey Lens (2024)  argues that AI is increasingly becoming a strategic imperative for
businesses. According to the authors, AI is not merely a tool but a transformative force that
redefines how businesses operate and deliver value. They emphas ize that AI-driven analytics
can identify patterns overlooked by humans, leading to optimized resource allocation and
improved agility, which is critical in retail, finance, and manufacturing (Survey Lens).
3. IIDE (2025)  in "AI in Business: Transforming Growth and Efficiency" explains that AI
refers to using machine learning, NLP, and RPA to optimize operations. The study notes that
AI solves major pain points such as inefficient manual processes and poor personalization.
The author points out that AI adoption across business functions has more than doubled in
recent years, with chatbots and automation freeing employees for strategic work (IIDE).
Artificial Intelligence (Ai) In Business
Artificial Intelligence (AI) in busi ness is defined as the use of AI technologies "such
as machine learning, natural language processing, generative AI, predictive analytics and
computer vision to automate work, optimize operations, improve decision -making and drive
business value". The concept has evolved from simple pilot projects focused on automation
to a strategic imperative. AI is not merely a technological tool but a "transformative force that
redefines how businesses operate, compete, and deliver value to customers". The modern
concept involves building intelligent systems that can process vast amounts of data, derive
actionable insights, and automate complex processes (Survey  Lens). Importance of AI in
Modern Business:
Enhances Decision-Making:
The strategic importance of AI lies in its ability to process vast data and empower
businesses to respond swiftly to market dynamics. AI systems analyze both structured and
unstructured datasets to uncover actionable insights that traditional analysis might miss.
Improve Productivity and Profitability:
The adoption of AI across business functions has more than doubled, with significant
productivity gains (IIDE). According to research from the IBM Institute for Business Value,
79% of executives report that AI has improved productivity and will c ontribute significantly
to revenue by 2030 .  Drives Operational Efficiency: AI enables organizations to "streamline
operations, reduce costs, and minimize errors" through automation and AI -driven analytics
(Survey Lens). This agility is critical for mainta ining competitiveness in retail, finance, and
manufacturing sectors.  AI is fundamentally transforming traditional commerce from
transaction-based models to intelligent, experience-driven ecosystems.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 333

Transformation of Customer Experience:
 AI solves busines s pain points like "inefficient manual processes, poor customer
personalization, and slow data analysis" (IIDE). Businesses use NLP -powered chatbots and
recommendation engines to provide hyper -personalized service. For instance, AI "powers
chatbots that enhance customer support" and allows businesses to understand customer needs
better through faster data analysis (IIDE).
 Transformation of Operations and Supply Chain:
AI technologies automate repetitive tasks and help organizations make smarter, data -
driven decisions. AI can "quickly detect anomalies to prevent fraud or system failures, reduce
risks for businesses" and streamlines workflows, freeing employees for strategic activities
(IIDE).
Emergence of New Business Models:
 AI is not just improving existing processes but enabling new ways of value creation.
The ability to "Analyze structured and unstructured datasets" and "Improve forecasting" and
"Aid real -time decision making" has led to models like conversational commerce  and
predictive retail.
Conclusion
The present study concludes that Artificial Intelligence has become a fundamental
pillar of modern business and commerce. Initially started as simple automation tools, AI has
now evolved into a strategic force that decide s how businesses operate, compete, and create
value for customers.  The study found that AI is transforming every aspect of traditional
business. In marketing and customer service, it enables personalized recommendations and
24/7 support through chatbots. In operations and supply chain, it helps in demand forecasting,
inventory optimization, and reducing costs. In overall business strategy, it helps with faster
and more accurate decision -making by analyzing large amounts of data.  It is concluded that
the future of commerce will not be about AI replacing humans, but AI augmenting human
capabilities. The businesses that will succeed in the future are those that effectively combine
human creativity and judgment with AI's speed and analytical power. While AI offe rs huge
opportunities, challenges like data privacy, high implementation cost, and the need for skilled
employees must be addressed.

STRENGTHENING RURAL BRANDS IN INDIA: CONSUMER TRUST, DIGITAL
MARKETING AND DISTRIBUTION STRATEGIES
                            R. DEEPAK KUMAR & B.A. SAI PRASANTH
                                                        B.COM (CA)
                          SRIRAM COLLEGE OF ARTS AND SCIENCE
Abstract
Brand management and rural marketing are important areas for businesses seeking
sustainable growth in India. Rural markets represent a large and diverse consumer base with
changing needs, rising purchasing power, better connectivity, increasing digital adoption and
greater exposure to brands. Effective brand management helps companies build trust, create a
clear identity, maintain consistent quality and develop long-term customer relationships.
Keywords
Brand Management - Rural Marketing - Consumers- Behaviour- Loyalty- Digital
Marketing, Distribution- Market Opportunities- India.
Introduction
India has a large rural population and rural areas continue to offer significant
opportunities for  businesses. Changes in income, education, mobile connectivity, digital
payments, transportation and media access are influencing rural consumption patterns. Rural
consumers are becoming more aware of national, regional and local brands and increasingly
compare products based on price, quality, trust and usefulness. Therefore, companies need
marketing approaches that are different from those used in urban markets.
Objectives of Study
1.The seminar aims to explain the role of brand management in developing customer
trust and brand loyalty in rural markets.
2.It seeks to identify major rural marketing opportunities in India and understand the
changing behaviour of rural consumers.
Review Of Literature
Kotler and Keller (2016)  Explained that effective brand management helps companies
create a strong brand identity, build customer trust and develop long -term brand loyalty.
These ideas are also relevant to rural markets, where familiarity and t rust can influence
consumer decisions.
Krishnamacharyulu and Ramakrishnan (2011)  Highlighted the importance of
understanding rural consumers, local markets, distribution systems and regional differences.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 336

Their study shows that companies need suitable marke ting strategies to reach rural customers
effectively.
Kashyap (2012)  Discussed the growing opportunities in rural markets and emphasized the
importance of affordable products, appropriate distribution and understanding local consumer
needs. Rural markets can provide significant opportunities for businesses when strategies are
adapted to local conditions.
Ramaswamy and Namakumari (2018)  Emphasized the importance of consumer behaviour,
market segmentation and effective communication in developing successful m arketing
strategies. Their work supports the need for businesses to understand differences among rural
consumers.
Opresnik ed al., (2020)  Explained that digital technology and changing consumer behaviour
are transforming marketing practices. In rural India , increasing mobile and internet usage
provides new opportunities for digital communication, customer engagement and market
expansion.
Importance of brand management in rural markets
Brand management creates a strong and recognizable identity for a product  or service.
In rural markets, trust and familiarity can strongly influence purchase decisions. Companies
can build brands by maintaining consistent quality, offering reliable service, using simple
communication and understanding local preferences. Regiona l languages, culturally relevant
messages and community -level engagement can make a brand more meaningful to rural
consumers. Strong branding can also help companies differentiate their products in
competitive markets.
Rural marketing opportunities in India
Rural India provides opportunities across consumer goods, banking, insurance,
healthcare, education, agriculture, retail, telecommunications, e -commerce and digital
services. Growing smartphone usage and internet access are connecting rural consumers wit h
online information, payments and shopping platforms. Companies can use a combination of
physical distribution and digital channels to improve market reach. Affordable product sizes,
flexible pricing, local retailers, self-help groups, rural entrepreneurs and regional promotional
activities can support wider adoption.
Changing Rural Consumer Behaviour
Rural consumers are not a single homogeneous group. Their preferences vary
according to region, occupation, income, education, age, culture and access to inf rastructure.
Increasing exposure to television, social media and online content has expanded product
awareness. Consumers are becoming more quality conscious while continuing to consider
affordability and value. Businesses therefore need market research an d customer
segmentation instead of treating all rural customers in the same way.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 337

Role Of Digital Technology
Digital technology has created new ways for brands to communicate with rural
consumers. Smartphones, social media, digital payments, online marketpl aces and messaging
platforms can improve awareness and convenience. Digital campaigns in regional languages
can support product education and customer engagement. At the same time, companies
should consider digital literacy, network availability and trust when designing rural digital
strategies.
Challenges in Rural Marketing
Rural marketing can involve challenges such as dispersed settlements, transportation
difficulties, limited storage and retail infrastructure, seasonal income, price sensitivity and
differences in language and culture. Companies may also face difficulties in building
awareness and maintaining last -mile availability. Effective planning requires suitable
distribution systems, local partnerships, reliable communication and continuous
understanding of customer needs.
Strategies for Effective Rural Brand Management
Companies can strengthen rural brands by offering dependable quality, affordable and
useful products, suitable pack sizes and region -specific communication. Building strong
relationships with local retailers and community networks can improve availability and trust.
Companies can also combine traditional media with digital channels and use customer
feedback to improve products and services. Long -term commitment to rural marke ts is
important because brand loyalty develops through consistent value and positive customer
experience.
Conclusion
Brand management and rural marketing are closely connected to the growth
opportunities available in India. Rural consumers are increasingly  connected, informed and
open to products that provide quality, value and convenience. Although rural markets present
infrastructure, affordability and diversity -related challenges, they also provide opportunities
for innovation and inclusive business grow th. A customer -centric and locally relevant
approach can help brands build trust and create sustainable presence in rural India.
References
1. Kotler, P., & Keller, K. L. — Marketing Management, Pearson Education.
2. Kotler, P., Armstrong, G., & Opresnik, M. O. — Principles of Marketing, Pearson.
3. Ramaswamy, V. S., & Namakumari, S. — Marketing Management: Indian
Context Global Perspective, Macmillan.
4. Krishnamacharyulu, C. S. G., & Ramakrishnan, L. — Rural Marketing: Text and
Cases, Pearson.

and rural markets in India.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 338

A Study on Consumer Preference towards Quick Commercial App among the College
Students

S. AKALYA
B. Com (CA)
               Sriram College of Arts and Science, Perumalpattu
Abstract
Quick commerce is a fast -growing part of e-commerce that allows customers to order
groceries, snacks, personal care items, and other daily -use products through mobile apps and
receive them within a short time. Apps such as Zepto, Blinkit, and Swiggy Instamart are
popular among young digital users because they provide convenience, quick delivery, digital
payment options, and attractive offers. This study examines the preference of college students
towards quick commerce apps and identifies the factors that influence their choice. The study
focuses on delivery speed, product availability, price, discounts, delivery charges, payment
options, and overall service quality. Primary data is col lected from college students through a
structured Google Form questionnaire.
Keywords: Quick Commerce - E-Commerce-Consumer-Preference-College Students, -
Digital Marketing.
Introduction
E-commerce has changed the way people buy products and services. Customers can
search, compare, order, and pay through websites and mobile applications without visiting a
physical store. Digital marketing supports online shopping through social media, onli ne
advertisements, search engines, offers, notifications, and personalised recommendations.
Quick commerce is a recent development in e -commerce that mainly focuses on very fast
delivery of daily -use products. College students are an important group becaus e they
regularly use smartphones, digital payments, social media, and online shopping apps. Their
preference may depend on delivery time, price, discounts, product availability, delivery
charges, product quality, and trust.
Objectives of the Study
1.The main objective is to examine the preference of college students towards quick
commerce apps.
2.The study also aims to identify commonly used apps, understand the products students
usually order, examine the importance of delivery speed, price, discounts,  delivery charges
and payment options, and measure overall satisfaction.
Scope of the Study
The study focuses on college students and their experience with quick commerce apps
such as Zepto, Blinkit, Swiggy Instamart, and similar services. It covers consum er
preference, buying behaviour, satisfaction, and factors influencing app selection. It does not
measure the financial performance of quick commerce companies.

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 339

Review of Literature
 Kumar and Singh (2022)  Studied consumer behaviour towards online grocery
delivery services. The study found that convenience, time -saving, product availability, and
delivery speed were major factors influencing consumers’ preference for online delivery
platforms.
 Priya and Raj (2023)  Studied online shopping preferences among c ollege students.
The study revealed that students preferred mobile -based shopping because of easy access,
attractive offers, affordable prices, and convenience.
Quick Commerce Usage and Preference
i. Preference Towards Quick Commerce
 College students prefer quick commerce apps because they provide a convenient and
easy way to purchase products through mobile applications. The availability of products, easy
ordering process and quick delivery make these apps useful for students. Their preference
mainly depends on convenience, service quality and their individual purchasing needs.
ii. Usage of Quick Commerce Apps
 Quick commerce apps are increasingly used by college students for purchasing
products without visiting physical stores. Students can place orders a t their convenience and
receive products within a short period. The ease of accessing these apps through smartphones
makes them suitable for students.
iii. Commonly Used Quick Commerce Apps
 College students use different quick commerce applications accord ing to their
convenience and requirements. The commonly used apps can be identified through the
responses collected from the students. This helps to understand the level of preference for
different quick commerce platforms.
iv. Preferred Payment Options
Payment options provide convenience while purchasing through quick commerce apps.
Students may use different payment methods such as UPI, debit cards, credit cards or cash on
delivery. The availability of convenient payment options can improve the purchasi ng
experience.
v. Overall Satisfaction Towards Quick Commerce Apps
 Overall satisfaction reflects students' experience with quick commerce services. It can
be influenced by delivery speed, product availability, price, discounts, delivery charges and
payment convenience. Measuring satisfaction helps to understand how students evaluate their
overall experience with quick commerce apps.

This survey looks at how college students use quick commerce delivery apps. Most of the
students surveyed are females aged 19 to 20 years. Every student who took part in the survey

National Level Seminar on Application of Artificial Intelligence in
Commerce and Management
ISBN:978-81-180170-6-6                 DOI:10.65647/msp/141/2026 Page 341

actively uses these delivery apps. Among all apps, Zepto is the most popular choice, followed
by Swiggy. Most students do not place orders every day. They usually order items only once
a week or whenever needed. Snacks and drinks are the most common things students order.
They also use these apps to buy personal care items and b asic groceries. Overall, quick
commerce apps help students get their everyday essentials and snacks delivered instantly.
Suggestions
 Quick commerce apps such as Zepto, Blinkit, Swiggy Instamart  and other similar
platforms should consider expanding their services to rural and semi -urban areas. At present,
these services are mainly available in cities and areas where nearby stores and delivery
facilities are available. By increasing their delivery  coverage and establishing more local
stores or delivery centres in rural areas, more people can benefit from quick commerce
services. This will help make quick commerce apps accessible to a wider range of consumers.
Conclusion
Quick commerce is an importa nt part of the changing e -commerce environment. It
provides a quick and convenient way to buy daily -use products through mobile applications.
For college students, these apps can reduce shopping time and provide simple digital payment
options. However, consumer preference depends on several factors, including delivery speed,
price, offers, product availability, delivery charges, product quality, and trust. The survey will
provide a clearer understanding of student preference and satisfaction towards quick
commerce apps.
